Supports thread reply detection for multi-turn review conversations. Requires the el event listener plugin and a GitHub repository with CodeRabbit enabled. - **claude-code-el-sendgrid**: SendGrid inbound email event source for the el plugin. Receive and process incoming emails via SendGrid Inbound Parse webhook as background events in Claude Code. Requires the el event listener plugin and a SendGrid account with Inbound Parse configured. ([mividtim/claude-code-el-sendgrid](https://github.com/mividtim/claude-code-el-sendgrid))
- **claude-code-el-slack**: Slack event source for the el (event-listeners) plugin. Enables Claude Code agents to respond to Slack channel activity in real time without polling. Pairs with ngrok for public webhook URLs. ([mividtim/claude-code-el-slack](https://github.com/mividtim/claude-code-el-slack))
